## 2026-10-26 Current: derived eGFR trend card (awaiting user confirmation)

- Adult CKD-EPI 2021 creatinine estimate is display-only and uses exact creatinine, age at test date, and sex; it does not use weight. Missing inputs, minors, comparisons, and unknown units are not guessed.
- Reuses generic trend search, grouping, pin, time-range, chart, preview, hospital color, and source-point detail behavior. Derived identity remains separate from hospital-reported eGFR and never rewrites reports.
- Push main for review and compile/unit validation only. Do not change release/ready.json or publish before user confirmation.
